## 3.1.28 M3.2b — FlashAttention-2 (streaming online softmax) — CORE scalar/register kernel [MERGED]

The M3 campaign deeply proved the **GEMM** path (M2.6→M3.8 Q4_K_M coopmat matmul, now callable from PyTorch via the M4.1 zero-copy frontend). **M3.2b broadens the thesis beyond GEMM to ATTENTION** — the OTHER flagship LLM kernel — and delivers the **online-softmax** primitive. The point: **ONE annotated `.axc` source expresses the FlashAttention-2 algorithm** (tiled, numerically-stable STREAMING softmax, **no full S=QKᵀ materialization**) → portable SPIR-V → correct on the GPU.

**FlashAttention-2 recurrence.** For Q[N,d], K[M,d], V[M,d] → O[N,d], instead of materializing S=QKᵀ [N,M] (O(N·M) memory), tile over K/V and maintain per query row a numerically-stable ONLINE softmax: running max `m_i` (init −∞), running denom `l_i` (init 0), accumulator `acc[d]` (init 0). For each K/V block j: `s = Q_i·K_jᵀ · 1/√d`; `m_new = max(m_i, s)`; `correction = exp(m_i − m_new)`; `p = exp(s − m_new)`; `l_i = l_i·correction + p`; `acc = acc·correction + p·V_j`; `m_i = m_new`. Finalize `O_i = acc / l_i`. The rescale-the-accumulator trick avoids materializing S and is numerically stable (every exp arg ≤ 0 because m is the running max).

**This supersedes `examples/tiled_attention.axc`** — whose own header (line 2) declares it the C1 NON-streaming baseline and FA2 *"deferred to M3.2b."* M3.2b IS that C2.

**PURE SOURCE — NO codegen feature (the load-bearing decision, cited from `body.rs`).** The new kernel `examples/flash_attention.axc` adds **ZERO compiler changes**; it is built entirely on machinery `tiled_attention.axc` already exercises:
- **Loop-carried scalars (`m_i`, `l_i`) need NO OpPhi.** Scalar bindings get a Function-storage `OpVariable` in the prelude (`body.rs:467-473`); an `Assign` is an `OpStore` (`body.rs:524-540`), a read an `OpLoad` — so a `let mut` mutated inside the K/V loop survives across iterations **through memory**, exactly as `tiled_attention.axc`'s `max_score`/`denom` (lines 53, 83) already do (AT-1630 bit-exact). The OpPhi loop-carry in `emit_for_range` is **COOPMAT-ONLY** (`detect_loop_carried_coopmat`, `body.rs:1044`; phi insertion `body.rs:1101-1103, 1178-1259`) and is **inert** for a no-coopmat kernel. **The M3.3 OpPhi machinery is correctly NOT invoked.**
- **The `acc[d]` accumulator is a `shared[f32,64]` array**, written `acc[d] = acc[d]*correction + p*v_tile[d]` inside the loop — the proven `k_tile[d] = K[...]` shared-write pattern (`tiled_attention.axc:59-61`). With `@workgroup(1,1,1)` (one invocation per query row, `q_row = gid(0)`) there is **no cross-invocation race** on `acc` — it is this invocation's scratch held in shared, persisting across iterations through workgroup memory.

**exp decision: reuse Taylor `1+x+x²/2` — there is NO exp builtin.** Confirmed by grep across `axc-codegen/hir/parser/lexer`: the only builtins are the 4 coopmat (`axc-hir/src/coopmat.rs:154-182`) and 4 q4_0; **no `OpExtInstImport "GLSL.std.450"` / OpExtInst Exp is emitted anywhere** (the `GLSL450` token is only the memory-model enum). FA2 evaluates exp at args **≤ 0 by construction** (`s − m_new ≤ 0`, `m_i − m_new ≤ 0`; both `= 0` at the running-max row → exp `= 1`, Taylor exact). The FROZEN tolerance is measured vs a CPU oracle using the **IDENTICAL** Taylor formula (the kernel and oracle compute the SAME function — apples-to-apples, AT-1630 design), and the fixtures keep post-max args in Taylor's faithful region so the result is ALSO a true attention. A true `exp` builtin (OpExtInst GLSL.std.450 Exp) is a clean **M3.2c** follow-on for wide-range real LLM logits.

**⚠ Taylor near-uniform LIMITATION — HARD-WIRED + DISCLOSED (r2, honest scalar-core envelope).** The M3.2b scalar core uses a Taylor exp (`1+x+x²/2`) accurate **ONLY for near-uniform attention (post-max logit gaps ≲ 0.7)**; real attention logit spreads (−10..−50) need a true exp builtin = **M3.2c**. *Numerical trace* (Taylor vs true exp, arg always ≤ 0): x=−0.7 ~3% off; x=−2 ~7×; x=−5 ~1262×; x=−20 Taylor=+181 while true≈2e‑9 (i.e. diverges UPWARD → garbage / negative weights past ~−2). So the kernel is a **faithful** attention only when ALL post-max exp args stay in ~[−0.7, 0]. This is (i) **DISCLOSED** verbatim in the `examples/flash_attention.axc` header comment, and (ii) **HARD-WIRED** by the tests: every fixture is built in the faithful band (inheriting AT-1630's Q/K scale → post-max args in [−0.68, 0]) and a helper `fa2_fixture_max_postmax_arg` replays the recurrence to assert the actual `max |post-max arg| ≤ 0.7` over the whole fixture — so a wide-spread fixture cannot self-consistently pass GPU-vs-oracle while computing a NON-faithful attention. **The DELIVERABLE of M3.2b is the streaming online-softmax ALGORITHM expressed + run correctly, NOT production-range attention** (which is M3.2c's true-exp builtin). No spin — this is a known scalar-core limitation, enforced and disclosed, not hidden.

**head_dim ≤ 64 — load-bearing precondition (r2).** `acc`/`k_tile`/`v_tile` are `shared[f32,64]`; `head_dim > 64` would OOB them (UB / robustness-clamp → silently wrong). A host-side preflight `fa2_preflight(head_dim)` asserts `head_dim ≤ 64` (clear message, raise/skip) **before** every dispatch; the bound is documented as a kernel precondition. Larger `head_dim` is M3.2c. The tests use `head_dim` = 64 (AT-1740/1741) and a 32 cross-check (AT-1740b).

**The kernel (`examples/flash_attention.axc`).** Q/K/V/O each [seq_len, head_dim], head_dim ≤ 64 (the `shared[f32,64]` bound). Dispatch `(seq_len,1,1)`, `@workgroup(1,1,1)`, `q_row=gid(0)`. K/V **block size B_c = 1** (the simplest correct streaming form — algorithmically identical to FA2 tiling at the degenerate B_c=1; with wg=(1,1,1) there is no intra-block parallelism to exploit, so B_c>1 pays only with coopmat/multi-thread = M3.2c). State: `shared k_tile/v_tile/acc: shared[f32,64]`, `let mut m_i=-1e9f32`, `let mut l_i=0.0f32`. The **ONE subtle correctness point**: on the FIRST K/V iteration `m_i=-1e9` so `m_new=s` and the prior-mass correction must be **ZERO** (l_i and acc are 0 — no mass to rescale; `exp(-1e9-s)` under Taylor is astronomically wrong) — the kernel guards `if j==0u32 { correction = 0.0f32; }` and **the CPU oracle uses the IDENTICAL guard** (AT-1744).

**The streaming property — NO S anywhere (the whole point; r2 made FALSIFIABLE on both axes).** Shared footprint = `k_tile + v_tile + acc = 3·head_dim·4 = 768 B` per workgroup, **INDEPENDENT of seq_len**; AND exactly **4 global buffers** are bound (`Q/K/V/O`, each O(N·d)) with **NO O(N·M) or O(N) scratch/score buffer**. A materialized-S kernel needs O(seq_len) score storage per query (8 KB/query at seq_len=2048), either in shared or in a global scratch buffer. **AT-1742 asserts BOTH:** *(i)* `meta.shared_memory_bytes == 3·head_dim·4` (asserted symbolically, not just `==768`) and **INVARIANT to seq_len** (computed at seq_len=64 AND 2048) — proves **no shared-memory S**; *(ii)* `meta.binding_plan.buffers` is exactly `{Q,K,V,O}` (`len()==4`, names match), none O(N·M) — proves **no global-memory S**. *Note (r2):* `shared_memory_bytes` is a compile-time Σ of declared shared-array sizes (`axc-driver/src/lib.rs:144-152`) and `seq_len` is a runtime push-constant (never an array dim), so the shared check alone is structurally `==768` for ANY kernel — it proves only no-shared-S. The **binding-count check** (`binding_plan.buffers`, `param.rs:119-126`) is the second, non-tautological falsifier that closes "no S **anywhere**." The long-sequence **AT-1741** at seq_len≥2048 completing at all is the corroborating runtime evidence.

**Correctness oracle + the TWO tolerances (r2 — gate vs independent sanity).** `cpu_flash_attention_reference` (in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs`, mirroring `cpu_tiled_attention_reference:90-136`) runs the SAME online recurrence in f32 with the IDENTICAL Taylor-exp and IDENTICAL `j==0` correction=0 guard (same statement order). **TOLERANCE 1 — THE FROZEN GATE: combined abs/rel 1e-3** per O element, kernel vs this Taylor-identical oracle (same scheme as AT-1630/AT-1606), **NEVER loosened** — failing it FAILS the milestone. Attention outputs are convex combinations of V (weights ∈ [0,1], sum 1) — not cancellation-prone — so 1e-3 is justified and ample (expected actual gate diff ~1e-5 from f32 reassociation). **TOLERANCE 2 — INDEPENDENT SANITY, ~5e-2 (NOT a gate):** **AT-1743** (r2) adds a THIRD reference `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference` using Rust **std `f32::exp`** in a stable full-softmax on the SAME near-uniform fixture, and asserts the kernel matches it within ~5e-2 abs/rel — because the kernel AND the FA2 oracle both use Taylor, a 1e-3 match validates only the rescale ALGEBRA, not the exp; the true-exp leg closes the "two identical-buggy Taylor impls agree" hole (the ~3% Taylor-vs-true gap at the band edge fits under 5e-2). AT-1743 ALSO keeps the algebra leg (online oracle vs stable full-softmax-Taylor, abs/rel ≤1e-4). **All legs use abs/rel tolerance, NOT `assert_eq`** (the rescale reassociates f32). To be explicit: **1e-3 kernel-vs-Taylor-oracle is THE gate; ~5e-2 kernel-vs-true-softmax is a distinct, looser, independent sanity check.**

**Acceptance (HONEST, measured NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000; Lavapipe CI fallback).** (a) compiles + spirv-val clean (caps `[Shader]` only, no extensions) — **AT-1738**; (b) within FROZEN 1e-3 of the FA2 oracle at SMALL (seq_len=64, d=64, **AT-1740**), a **head_dim=32 cross-check vs the C1 `tiled_attention` kernel on the SAME shape** (two algorithms, one answer, both within 1e-3 — **AT-1740b**), AND LONG (seq_len≥2048, **AT-1741** — proves streaming runs with O(tile) shared + 0 global scratch); (c) **no S anywhere** — `shared_memory_bytes==3·head_dim·4` INVARIANT to seq_len AND `binding_plan.buffers=={Q,K,V,O}` — **AT-1742**; (d) **oracle independence** — kernel vs Taylor oracle (FROZEN 1e-3 gate), kernel vs **true-exp** full-softmax (~5e-2 sanity), oracle vs full-softmax-Taylor (≤1e-4 algebra) — **AT-1743**; (e) `j==0`-guard + running-max-climb adversarial orderings (small monotone increments staying in the faithful band) — **AT-1744**; (f) **every fixture asserts `max |post-max arg| ≤ 0.7` and passes `fa2_preflight(head_dim≤64)`**; (g) honest latency/throughput REPORTED, **explicitly labeled SLOW / non-competitive (scalar core, 1 invocation/query row). NO perf gate.** The bar is CORRECTNESS + the STREAMING online-softmax algorithm expressed in one annotated source.

**Phasing.** **M3.2b CORE (this milestone):** scalar/register FA2, B_c=1, wg=(1,1,1), Taylor-exp, pure source, no coopmat — proves the online-softmax algorithm expresses + runs correctly + streams. **M3.2c (deferred):** coopmat-accelerated QKᵀ and PV (S_ij and acc+=P_ij·V_j as cooperative_matrix matmuls), multi-thread query tiles (wg>1, which makes `acc`-in-shared a cross-thread accumulator needing a different layout — flagged), B_c>1 with a `@strategy` hole, and optionally an `exp` builtin for wide-range scores — **this is where perf is pursued.**

**Top risks.** (1) the first-iteration correction guard (the −1e9 init) — the single subtle correctness point, mitigated by AT-1744 + identical oracle guard + AT-1743. (2) Taylor range — real LLM logits can drive post-max args < −10 where Taylor is wildly wrong; the CORE fixtures STAY in the faithful region and this is disclosed as a scalar-core LIMITATION resolved by an M3.2c exp builtin, not hidden. (3) acc-in-shared assumes wg=(1,1,1) (single owner, no race) — M3.2c multi-thread must NOT naively reuse this layout. (4) perf is expectedly ≪1 TFLOPS — acceptable; the only risk is misreading the milestone as a perf claim, which the acceptance explicitly forecloses.

*(Status: PROPOSED · r2 — Architect. Design spec → `.pipeline/runs/M3.2b-architect.json` (verdict PROPOSED, revision r2). r1 reviews: optimistic APPROVE, pessimistic NEEDS_REVISION — core SOUND (loop-carry proven vs AT-1630; Taylor limitation honestly disclosed) with four FIXABLE gaps. r2 closes them WITHOUT changing the kernel/algorithm: **(a)** AT-1742 gains a no-global-scratch `binding_plan.buffers=={Q,K,V,O}` assertion (the shared==768 check alone is structurally tautological); **(b)** AT-1743 gains a true-exp `std::f32::exp` reference (~5e-2 sanity) distinct from the FROZEN 1e-3 Taylor gate; **(c)** the Taylor near-uniform limitation is HARD-WIRED (`fa2_fixture_max_postmax_arg ≤ 0.7` per fixture) + DISCLOSED (kernel header + here); **(d)** a host `fa2_preflight(head_dim≤64)`. Folded optimistic notes: AT-1743 abs/rel (not assert_eq), a head_dim=32 cross-check (AT-1740b), and the `j==0` correction=0 guard as the SOLE first-iter mechanism. New artifacts: `examples/flash_attention.axc` (pure source, ZERO codegen change) + tests in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs` (cpu_flash_attention_reference + fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference + fa2_fixture_max_postmax_arg + fa2_preflight + fa2_assert_no_scratch + AT-1740/1740b/1741/1742/1743/1744) + a compile anchor in `compile_shared_examples.rs` (AT-1738). FROZEN 1e-3 gate UNCHANGED; streaming online-softmax algorithm UNCHANGED. Re-review.)*

## 3.1.29 M3.2c-exp — a real `exp()` builtin via SPIR-V GLSL.std.450 (the FIRST extended instruction) [MERGED]

**Implemented (M3.2c-exp).** Measured on NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell: GPU `exp(x)` vs `f32::exp(x)` over x∈[−30,5] **max rel diff = 7.09e-7** (≪ frozen 1e-3); `flash_attention_exp.axc` at real logit spreads (max post-max arg = 11.36) vs the true-exp softmax oracle **max_diff = 2.4e-7** (AT-1825); the M3.2b Taylor kernel on the SAME wide fixture is **max_diff = 0.124** ≫ 1e-3 (AT-1826 negative control). The GLSL.std.450 import is emitted **exactly once** on a ≥2-exp kernel (AT-1822, raw codegen scan) and the capability/extension set is **byte-identical** to M3.2b (AT-1823 — no new capability). M3.2b AT-1740/1740b/1741/1743/1744 still pass (AT-1827 no-regression). HONEST: correctness only, no perf gate (coopmat-accelerated attention = M3.2c-perf).


M3.2b (§3.1.28) proved the **FlashAttention-2 streaming online-softmax ALGORITHM**, but pinned it inside a **Taylor `1+x+x²/2` exp envelope** faithful ONLY for near-uniform attention (post-max logit gaps ≲ 0.7). Real LLM attention logit spreads (−10..−50) are garbage under Taylor (Taylor(−5)=1262× off; goes NEGATIVE past ~−2). **M3.2c-exp removes that limitation** by adding a real `exp(x: f32) -> f32` builtin lowering to the SPIR-V **GLSL.std.450 extended instruction `Exp` (=27)** — the **FIRST `OpExtInstImport "GLSL.std.450"` + `OpExtInst … Exp`** ever emitted by the codegen. Real-range attention becomes NUMERICALLY CORRECT.

**The builtin surface.** `exp(x: f32) -> f32` — a value-returning free-function call in expression position (`let p: f32 = exp(px);`), modeled VERBATIM on the M3.5 `f32_to_f16(x: f32) -> f16` precedent (the closest existing unary-f32 builtin). Like the q4_0 builtins, `exp(...)` parses as a generic `Expr::Call { name:"exp", args }` (it returns a value, so it reaches `check_call` in expression position — NOT the statement-level builtin dispatch at `parser.rs:1260` which is only for void-returning subgroup/coopmat_store). **NO parser change.** Scope: `exp` ONLY this milestone (the FlashAttention need); the GLSL.std.450 import machinery is designed to be REUSED for future ext-inst builtins (exp2=29, log=28, sqrt=31, etc. — explicitly extensible, but OUT OF SCOPE here — no feature without a test, anti-pattern #4-adjacent discipline).

**The lexer→codegen touch points (cited file:line, modeled on `f32_to_f16`):**
- **HIR data** — a new `Exp` variant on an `ExtInstBuiltin` enum (NEW module `crates/axc-hir/src/ext_inst.rs`, mirroring `q4_0.rs:28-132` in layout/discipline: `source_name()`/`from_source_name()`/`arity()=1`/`return_ty()=F32` + a `RESERVED_EXT_INST_BUILTIN_NAMES=["exp"]` sorted slice + `is_reserved_ext_inst_builtin()`, exactly like `q4_0.rs:140-153`). A NEW `HirExprKind::ExtInstBuiltin { op, args }` variant on `crates/axc-hir/src/expr.rs:190` (alongside `Q4_0Builtin`). **NO `buf_param_index`** — `exp` takes a scalar value, not a buffer.
- **Typecheck** — a new `check_ext_inst_call(tc, op, …)` in `crates/axc-hir/src/typecheck.rs` (modeled on `check_q4_0_call` at `typecheck.rs:3197-3375`, specifically the `F32ToF16` arm at `:3353-3373`): arity-1 check, arg must typecheck to `F32` (else a new `TypecheckError::ExpArgMustBeF32 { got_ty, span }` mirroring `F32ToF16ArgMustBeF32` at `:520`), result `ScalarTy::F32`. Dispatched FIRST in `check_call` (`typecheck.rs:3402-3404`, before q4_0/coopmat/subgroup — same pattern). The reserved-name guard at `typecheck.rs:931-937` gains an `is_reserved_ext_inst_builtin` clause so `let exp = …` is rejected (mirrors `ReservedQ4_0BuiltinName`).
- **lower.rs** — the `find_mul_add_in_expr` exhaustiveness scan at `crates/axc-hir/src/lower.rs:600-602` gains an `ExtInstBuiltin { args, .. }` arm (scan args, like the `Q4_0Builtin` arm) so the match stays exhaustive.
- **Codegen** — a new `emit_exp(b, tc, glsl450_set, x_id) -> Word` in a NEW `crates/axc-codegen/src/ext_inst.rs` (mirroring `q4_0.rs:207-222` `emit_f32_to_f16`). Dispatched from a NEW `HirExprKind::ExtInstBuiltin` arm in `body.rs` (alongside the `Q4_0Builtin` arm at `body.rs:1487`): `let x_id = emit_expr(em, &args[0])?; let set = em.glsl450_set_id(); q::emit_exp(em.b, em.type_cache, set, x_id)`.

**THE KEY CORRECTNESS POINT — the GLSL.std.450 import is emitted ONCE per module (cached id).** GLSL.std.450 is an extended instruction set: it requires `OpExtInstImport "GLSL.std.450"` ONCE in the module header (`rspirv` `Builder::ext_inst_import(name) -> Word`, which appends to `module.ext_inst_imports` — the correctly-ordered header section), and the returned set-id is reused by EVERY `OpExtInst`. Emitting the import per-`exp`-call would produce **multiple OpExtInstImport of the same set** — wasteful and a spirv-val target. **The cached id lives in `ScalarTypeCache`** (`body.rs:130-137`) as a new `glsl450_set_id: Option<Word>` field with a `get_or_emit_glsl450_set(&mut self, b) -> Word` accessor (lazy `Option` → emit-once-then-cache, EXACTLY the `u32_const_cache`/`scalar_ids` dedup pattern at `body.rs:145-165,182-190`). `ScalarTypeCache` is created ONCE per kernel at `emit.rs:188` and threaded through `emit_kernel_body` (`body.rs:430`) — so the cache spans the whole module body. The first `exp` call emits the import + caches; every subsequent `exp` reuses the cached set-id. **Test AT-1822 asserts EXACTLY ONE `OpExtInstImport` in a kernel with MULTIPLE exp calls.**

**The SPIR-V emission.** Per `exp(x)`: `%r = OpExtInst %f32 %glsl450_set 27 %x` via `rspirv` `Builder::ext_inst(f32_ty, None, set_id, 27, [Operand::IdRef(x_id)])` (the autogen API at `rspirv-0.12.0 dr/build/mod.rs:598`; instruction-number `27` = GLSL.std.450 `Exp`, passed as the `LiteralExtInstInteger`). `f32_ty` from `tc.scalar_id(b, ScalarTy::F32)`. The import itself: `Builder::ext_inst_import("GLSL.std.450")` (`mod.rs:509`).

**NO new SPIR-V capability — CONFIRMED.** GLSL.std.450 is an EXTENDED INSTRUCTION SET (an `OpExtInstImport` in the module header), NOT a capability and NOT an `OpExtension`. `OpExtInst … Exp` on an `f32` operand needs only `Shader` (already declared) + the `Float` type (already used everywhere). So `CapabilitiesRequired` (`body.rs:195-246`) gains **NOTHING** — `emit_exp` takes NO `&mut CapabilitiesRequired` (unlike `emit_f32_to_f16` which sets `float16`). The import is module-header state on the `Builder`, distinct from the capability-union machinery in `emit.rs`. **spirv-val must accept** the import + `OpExtInst Exp` with zero capability changes (AT-1823).

**The real-range FlashAttention re-validation.** A NEW kernel **`examples/flash_attention_exp.axc`** — the M3.2b `flash_attention.axc` VERBATIM except the two Taylor lines become real `exp()` calls:
- `let mut correction: f32 = exp(cx);` (was `1.0f32 + cx + cx*cx*0.5f32`) — the `j==0` `correction = 0.0f32` guard is RETAINED VERBATIM (the −1e9 first-iter mechanism is orthogonal to the exp implementation; `exp(m_i - m_new)` at j==0 is never used).
- `let p: f32 = exp(px);` (was the Taylor px form).
Keeping a **NEW** kernel (not editing `flash_attention.axc`) preserves M3.2b's Taylor kernel + AT-1740/1743/1744 as the **near-uniform regression** (no regression, AT-1827). The new kernel **LIFTS the ≤0.7 Taylor faithful-band fixture bound** — it is tested at REAL attention logit spreads (post-max args spanning −10..0, the regime Taylor was garbage) and the `fa2_fixture_max_postmax_arg ≤ 0.7` hard-wire is REMOVED for this kernel's fixtures (replaced by a wide-spread fixture builder).

**The correctness oracle + FROZEN tolerance.** The oracle is **`fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference`** (already in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs:1435-1476`, uses Rust std `f32::exp`) — now **apples-to-apples**: GPU GLSL.std.450 `Exp` ≈ `f32::exp` to high accuracy, so the comparison is meaningful (vs M3.2b where the kernel-Taylor vs true-exp leg was a looser ~5e-2 SANITY check BECAUSE the kernel used Taylor). **FROZEN GATE: combined abs/rel `1e-3`** per O element, kernel-exp vs the true-exp softmax oracle — NEVER loosened. Justification: GLSL.std.450 `Exp` accuracy is implementation-defined but high (Vulkan requires ≤ a few ULP; NVIDIA's is near-IEEE); attention outputs are convex combinations of V (weights ∈[0,1], sum 1 — NOT cancellation-prone); the residual is GPU-exp-vs-`f32::exp` ULP differences amplified through the softmax normalize, comfortably ≪ 1e-3 (expected actual ~1e-5..1e-4). A **direct exp-correctness test (AT-1820/1821)** independently pins `exp(x)` on the GPU vs `f32::exp(x)` over x ∈ [−30, 5] (INCLUDING the x<−5 regime Taylor failed) within a tight rel/abs tol (1e-3, with the small-magnitude near-zero values abs-guarded) — this is the FIRST-class correctness gate on the builtin itself, separate from the attention kernel.

**Acceptance (measured on NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000).** (i) `exp` compiles + **spirv-val clean** — `OpExtInstImport "GLSL.std.450"` + `OpExtInst … Exp` emitted, **import emitted EXACTLY ONCE** even with multiple exp calls (AT-1822/1823/1824). (ii) GPU `exp(x)` matches `f32::exp(x)` within the tight tol over x∈[−30,5] incl. the Taylor-failure regime (AT-1820/1821). (iii) `flash_attention_exp.axc` produces CORRECT attention at REAL logit spreads (−10..−50 regime) within the FROZEN 1e-3 vs the true-exp softmax oracle — the regime M3.2b's Taylor kernel CANNOT do (AT-1825/1826). (iv) the M3.2b Taylor kernel + AT-1740/1743/1744 STILL pass — NO regression (AT-1827). **HONEST:** exp is now real-range correct; perf is STILL the scalar core (B_c=1, wg=(1,1,1)) — **NO perf gate**; coopmat-accelerated QKᵀ/PV is the M3.2c-perf follow-on (§3.1.28 phasing).

**Top risks.** (1) **import-once caching** — the load-bearing codegen-correctness point; mitigated by the `Option<Word>` lazy-cache in `ScalarTypeCache` (the proven `u32_const_cache` pattern) + AT-1822's exact-one-import assertion over a multi-exp kernel. (2) **`OpExtInst` result-type/operand correctness** — wrong result-type or a missing `IdRef` operand fails spirv-val; mitigated by AT-1823 (spirv-val) + the autogen `ext_inst` signature (`result_type, set, instruction, operands`). (3) **GLSL.std.450 `Exp` accuracy vs a tight tol** — implementation-defined; mitigated by the DIRECT exp-vs-`f32::exp` test (AT-1820) measuring the actual ULP gap, and the convex-combination structure of attention damping it; if the MEASURED gap somehow exceeds 1e-3 it is REPORTED, not hidden (the tol is frozen, not the claim). (4) **rspirv ext_inst API** — confirmed present (`ext_inst_import` `mod.rs:509`, `ext_inst` `mod.rs:598`) with the exact `(result_type, result_id, set, instruction, operands)` shape.

*(Status: PROPOSED — Architect. Design spec → `.pipeline/runs/M3.2c-exp-architect.json` (verdict PROPOSED). The FIRST GLSL.std.450 ext-inst in the codegen; machinery extensible to exp2/log/sqrt later (OUT OF SCOPE here). New artifacts: `crates/axc-hir/src/ext_inst.rs` (ExtInstBuiltin::Exp, mirroring q4_0.rs) + `HirExprKind::ExtInstBuiltin` + `check_ext_inst_call` (mirroring check_q4_0_call's F32ToF16 arm) + `ExpArgMustBeF32`; `crates/axc-codegen/src/ext_inst.rs` (emit_exp) + `ScalarTypeCache::glsl450_set_id` lazy import cache + the `body.rs` dispatch arm; `examples/flash_attention_exp.axc` (M3.2b VERBATIM, two Taylor lines → exp(), faithful-band bound LIFTED) + tests (AT-1820 exp-vs-f32::exp over [−30,5]; AT-1821 GPU; AT-1822 import-emitted-once; AT-1823 spirv-val + no-new-capability; AT-1824 compile anchor; AT-1825/1826 real-range FlashAttention vs true-exp oracle; AT-1827 M3.2b no-regression). FROZEN 1e-3 gate. NO new SPIR-V capability. NO parser change. NO perf gate (coopmat = M3.2c-perf).)*

## 3.1.30 M3.2c-perf — coopmat-accelerated FlashAttention-2 (QKᵀ on tensor cores + multi-thread query tile) [MERGED]

M3.2b (§3.1.28) + M3.2c-exp (§3.1.29) delivered a CORRECT, real-range FlashAttention-2 (`examples/flash_attention_exp.axc`): streaming online softmax, real `exp()`, but **SLOW** — scalar, one invocation per query row (`@workgroup(1,1,1)`), no tensor cores (~1.1 s at seq=2048). The GEMM campaign (M2.6→M3.8) proved coopmat tensor-core matmul deeply. **M3.2c-perf carries that win into attention: compute `S = Q·Kᵀ` (the score block) as a `cooperative_matrix` matmul, and switch from one-thread-per-query-row to a whole 16-row QUERY TILE per workgroup (32 lanes / one subgroup).** The bar is CORRECT + FASTER-than-our-scalar (an honest first attention-TFLOPS), NOT competitive-with-llama (that is a further follow-on + an A/B).

**THE CRUX — can a coopmat accumulator be rescaled by a per-row `correction` each block? — resolved FIRST.** FA2 step 6 is `acc = acc·correction + P·V`: the [TILE_Q, head_dim] output accumulator is rescaled by a per-ROW scalar each K/V block, THEN the P·V matmul is added. I READ the coopmat machinery: `coopmat_mul_add(a,b,c)` (`coopmat.rs:173`) emits `OpCooperativeMatrixMulAddKHR` = A·B+C ONLY — there is NO diag(scale)·C operand; a loop-carried coopmat accumulator may be reassigned only UNCONDITIONALLY (`detect_loop_carried_coopmat`, `body.rs:962`; conditional reassign → `LoopCarriedCoopMatConditionalReassignUnsupported`, `body.rs:1002`); and there is no coopmat→scalar elementwise-rescale builtin. **So rescaling a coopmat acc by a per-row scalar is NOT expressible — Option C (coopmat acc carried + per-row rescale) needs a NEW codegen feature (a coopmat diagonal/elementwise scale). DEFERRED.**

**DECISION — Option A (HYBRID, pure source, ZERO codegen change).** Keep `acc` in **`shared[f32, TILE_Q*head_dim]`** (NOT a coopmat). Then step 6 is an ORDINARY shared elementwise write — `acc[r*head_dim+d] = acc[r*head_dim+d]*correction[r] + Σ_t P[r][t]·V_j[t][d]` — the M3.2b acc-in-shared pattern (`flash_attention_exp.axc:120`) lifted from ONE row to a 16-row tile, parallelized over the 32 lanes. coopmat accelerates the **QKᵀ matmul** (step 1) — Q_i[16,head_dim]·K_jᵀ[head_dim,16]→S[16,16] via the proven shared-staged coopmat (`matmul_shared_coopmat.axc`), with the result written to shared via `coopmat_store`-to-shared (`coopmat.rs:358`, `emit_coopmat_store_shared_inline`, the M3.2 PART B path). The online softmax (rowmax, `exp()`→P, rowsum, correction) runs SCALAR over the shared S tile; **P·V stays SCALAR in CORE** (doing it coopmat would force `acc` into a coopmat → re-introduce the crux). Per-row `m_i`/`l_i` loop-carry through `shared[f32,16]` across the K/V loop (scalars never get OpPhi — `body.rs:1055` — exactly the M3.2b scalar-loop-carry, generalized to a tile).

**PURE SOURCE — the load-bearing decision (cited).** ZERO new builtin / codegen / capability / typecheck: coopmat S=QKᵀ (`coopmat_load`-from-shared `coopmat.rs:315`, `coopmat_mul_add` `:173`, `coopmat_store`-to-shared `:358`, `coopmat_zero`), `exp()` (M3.2c-exp), `shared[T,N]` (M3.2), `subgroup_invocation_id()`/`gid(axis)`/`workgroup_barrier()` — ALL existing. The crux is dissolved by keeping `acc` in shared. The S-accumulator is **re-zeroed per K/V block (NOT carried)** and the 4 head-dim sub-block `coopmat_mul_add`s are UNROLLED at the K/V-loop top level — this sidesteps the M3.6 nested-coopmat-carry silent-miscompile (`q4km_matmul_rb_coopmat_f32acc_cached.axc:24-27`) and the AT-1708 conditional-reassign trap by construction; the only loop-carried state across the K/V loop is `acc`/`m`/`l` in SHARED (no phi needed).

**Tiling + dispatch.** TILE_Q=TILE_K=16, head_dim=64 (load-bearing: =4 coopmat K-blocks + the shared bounds). `@workgroup(32,1,1)` (one subgroup, the proven coopmat WG size); dispatch `(seq_len/16, 1, 1)` workgroups; `q_tile=gid(0)/32`, `lane=subgroup_invocation_id()`. Q staged ONCE before the K/V loop (reused across all key blocks — the FA2 win); each workgroup streams all seq_len keys in 16-key blocks. **Shared = 13504 B = 13.19 KB** (q/k tiles f16 2048+2048 + v/s/acc f32 4096+1024+4096 + m/l/**corr** f32 64+64+**64**) < the 16384-byte portable floor — TIGHT (headroom ~2880 B); any layout growth (double-buffer/bigger tiles) is DEFERRED. The r1 budget OMITTED `corr_sh[16]` (+64 B); AT-2206 asserts the actual 13504 B incl. corr_sh. **Streaming preserved**: shared is INVARIANT to seq_len (seq is a push-constant, not an array dim) and exactly `{Q,K,V,O}` are bound (no O(seq) score buffer) — AT-2206.

**r2 — the FIRST per-lane DIVERGENT-work kernel: lane→cell partitions + barriers PINNED (race-freedom at the spec level).** This is the repo's first kernel where lanes do per-lane divergent scalar work (not symmetric cooperative staging), so r2 pins three things the Coder must not improvise. **R1 — BOTH work-partitions, DISJOINT + TOTAL:** (a) *softmax phase* (under `if lane < 16`) — lane L owns row L for L<16 (lanes 16..31 IDLE, the disclosed half-utilization ceiling); lane L is the SOLE writer of m_sh[L]/l_sh[L]/corr_sh[L] and of its own row band s_tile_sh[L·16..L·16+15] (P-overwrites-S in place); m_sh[L]/l_sh[L] are read+written by the SAME lane every K/V block → no cross-lane hazard on the loop-carried per-row state. (b) *P·V / zero-init / finalize phase* (all 32 lanes) — lane L owns cells {c : c = L + 32·i, i in 0..32} over the 1024 acc cells, with row r=c/64, col d=c%64; c mod 32 = L is unique → disjoint, and L+32·i covers [0,1024) exactly once → total (no acc cell uninitialized, the same partition zero-inits acc before the loop). **R2 — the 4 barriers, ALL at conditional_depth==0, NONE inside the lane<16 branch** (typecheck.rs:2498 BarrierInDivergentContext FORBIDS a barrier at depth>0 — the M3.7/M3.6-r2 rule): (1) after K/V (and first-block Q) staging → before the coopmat loads (RAW); (2) after the warp-collective coopmat S=QKᵀ store-to-shared (OpCooperativeMatrixStoreKHR, all 32 lanes) → before any scalar S-read (RAW — a miss = reading a partially-stored S = silent wrong, the M3.7 class); (3) after BOTH P (s_tile_sh, in place) AND **corr_sh** are fully written → before the P·V phase reads them — **THE key cross-lane visibility: corr_sh[r] is written by row-owner lane r but READ by a DIFFERENT cell-owner lane (c%32) in P·V, so this barrier MUST cover corr_sh, not only P** (the r1 wording was widened); (4) WAR barrier at end-of-body → before the next block overwrites k/v/s/corr shared. **R3 — s_acc is iteration-local (NO phi):** s_acc is DECLARED INSIDE the K/V loop body (fresh `coopmat_zero()` each iteration, the 4 head-dim `coopmat_mul_add`s UNROLLED at the K/V-body top level, NOT a nested loop) → it has NO pre-loop SSA id → `detect_loop_carried_coopmat` (body.rs:969) does NOT make it a phi → genuinely iteration-local, NOT carried across K/V blocks → the M3.6 nested-coopmat-carry silent-miscompile is sidestepped by construction. AT-2201 asserts the K/V loop header has EXACTLY 0 coopmat OpPhi (the falsifier for an accidental carry).

**Numerics — FROZEN 1e-3, real-range, vs the true-exp oracle.** Same FA2 online softmax + same real `exp()` as M3.2c-exp. The ONLY new error source: the QKᵀ dot is now an **f16-input coopmat matmul** (f16 A/B → f32 accumulator, the proven HMMA) vs the scalar f32 dot — ~1e-3-relative input rounding on the logits, which the softmax (a smooth bounded map; attention outputs are convex combinations of V, weights ∈[0,1] sum 1, not cancellation-prone) does not amplify catastrophically. FROZEN GATE: combined abs/rel **1e-3** per O element vs `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference` (the M3.2c-exp `std::f32::exp` oracle) at seq=512 (AT-2202) AND 2048 (AT-2203), head_dim=64, real logit spreads (post-max args −10..0). NEVER loosened — if f16-input QKᵀ exceeds 1e-3 it is REPORTED as the f16-input limitation (a distinct numerics honest-negative; fallback = scalar-f32 QKᵀ forfeiting the coopmat win), the gate is frozen not the claim.

**Acceptance (HONEST, measured NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000; Lavapipe coopmat-typed-skip).** PRIMARY (gating) = the CORRECTNESS + MECHANISM proof: (a) compiles + spirv-val clean, caps `[Shader, CooperativeMatrixKHR, VulkanMemoryModel]` + the GLSL.std.450 import once, OpPhi discipline (s_acc declared-inside-the-loop → EXACTLY 0 K/V-loop-header coopmat phis, R3), shared==13504 B incl corr_sh — **AT-2200/2201/2206**; (b) within FROZEN 1e-3 of the true-exp oracle at seq=512 AND 2048, head_dim=64 — **AT-2202/2203**; (c) cross-kernel agreement with the scalar `flash_attention_exp.axc` within 1e-3 at seq=512 AND 2048 incl a differing-rowmax fixture (two impls, one answer — the load-bearing structural-RACE detector for a wrong transpose/stride/rescale or a cross-lane race) — **AT-2205**. STRETCH (falsifiable, NOT primary): (d) **PERF GATE: ≥3× FASTER than the scalar kernel at seq=2048, head_dim=64**, GPU-resident, report the ratio + an honest attention-TFLOPS estimate + a per-phase breakdown — **AT-2204**. (e) M3.2b/M3.2c-exp no-regression — **AT-2207**. **NO false competitive-with-llama claim** — this is faster-than-OUR-scalar, an honest first attention-TFLOPS, NOT yet a llama flash-attention A/B.

**HONEST-NEGATIVE clause (M3.7/M3.8 discipline) — and it is the LIKELY outcome (Amdahl).** The ≥3× gate is a STRETCH, and per the pessimistic review's Amdahl analysis an honest-negative is the EXPECTED result: coopmat accelerates ONLY the small QKᵀ (step 1) while the scalar P·V (16 MACs/output over 1024 outputs, 32 lanes — the dominant ALU cost, un-accelerated), the 16-lane softmax (half-utilization + 2 exp/element), and the S→shared→scalar→P→shared→scalar-PV round-trips dominate → a realistic **~1.3-2× (or even no speedup)**. If the coopmat kernel does NOT beat the scalar kernel by 3×, the result is REPORTED AS A MEASURED NEGATIVE (both wall-times, the ratio, a QKᵀ-vs-softmax-vs-PV phase breakdown, a diagnosis) — a DOCUMENTED FINDING, **NOT a milestone failure**: the PRIMARY deliverable (correctness + the coopmat-QKᵀ + acc-in-shared MECHANISM, AT-2202/2203/2205) stands and de-risks the deferred Option C (coopmat P·V + diagonal-scale) where the real win lives. The FROZEN 1e-3 correctness gate and the 3× bar are NOT loosened to manufacture a win, and the scalar `flash_attention_exp.axc` REMAINS the leader. The scalar P·V is the prime follow-on target.

**Phasing.** **CORE (this milestone):** coopmat S=QKᵀ → shared + 32-lane TILE_Q=16 query tile + scalar online-softmax + scalar P·V + acc-in-shared rescale (Option A). **DEFERRED:** coopmat P·V with a coopmat acc (needs the diagonal-scale NEW codegen feature for per-row rescale — Option C, bounded follow-on); FP16 coopmat acc; TILE_Q>16 / multi-subgroup query tiles; head_dim>64; B_c>16 via `@strategy`; double-buffered staging; full occupancy tuning; the llama.cpp flash-attention A/B (only after coopmat-competitive).

**Top risks.** (1) THE CRUX — resolved by acc-in-shared, but if the scalar P·V + per-row rescale ERASES the coopmat QKᵀ win it becomes an honest-negative on AT-2204 (mitigated by the per-phase breakdown; coopmat P·V is the follow-on). (2) f16-input QKᵀ numerics — the ONLY new error source; could exceed 1e-3 (mitigated by convex-combination damping + reporting; fallback scalar-f32 QKᵀ). (3) M3.6-class coopmat-loop traps — AVOIDED by construction (re-zero+unroll S-acc → no nested carry; no conditional coopmat reassign; all barriers unconditional at conditional_depth==0). (4) S→shared→scalar-softmax→P→shared→scalar-PV data movement — extra shared traffic the pure-matmul kernels don't pay; may be the dominant cost (the honest-negative risk). (5) ≈13.5 KB shared is TIGHT (<16 KB floor) — no room for double-buffer/bigger tiles. (6) 16 active lanes in the softmax row-reduction = half-utilization (honest under-utilization, acceptable in CORE).

**IMPLEMENTED (measured, NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell).** `examples/flash_attention_coopmat.axc` ships PURE SOURCE (ZERO codegen change). spirv-val clean; the emitted capability set is a STRICT SUBSET of the proven `matmul_shared_coopmat.axc` kernel's caps (no NEW capability — code review verified the exact emitted set against the disassembly; the f16-shared coopmat staging caps, NOT Float16Buffer), GLSL.std.450 imported once; the K/V loop has **EXACTLY 0 coopmat OpPhi** (s_acc iteration-local; AT-2201 also pins the op shape: 3 coopmat types, 8 loads = 4 Q + 4 K, 1 S-store, 4 mul_adds, 1 OpConstantNull); shared == **13504 B** incl corr_sh, seq-invariant, buffers `{Q,K,V,O}` (AT-2206). **CORRECTNESS (PRIMARY): PASS** — vs the true-exp oracle, max combined abs/rel diff **8.5e-5 at seq=512** and **5.1e-5 at seq=2048** (both ≪ FROZEN 1e-3), on a DIFFERING-rowmax fixture (post-max args ~15, well past real LLM range) that exercises the cross-lane corr_sh write/read; cross-kernel agreement with the scalar `flash_attention_exp.axc` within ~8.5e-5 at seq=512 AND 2048 (AT-2202/2203/2205). The f16-input QKᵀ rounding (the only new error source) stays ~5–9e-5 — the convex-combination damping holds; the gate is not stressed. M3.2b + M3.2c-exp tests all still pass (AT-2207). **PERF (STRETCH ≥3×): MET — measured ~28.8× faster than the scalar kernel at seq=2048** (scalar 12.03 ms → coopmat 0.418 ms; attention-TFLOPS 0.089 → 2.57), GpuTimestamp resident. HONEST ATTRIBUTION: the large ratio is dominated NOT by coopmat QKᵀ alone but by the PARALLELISM change — the scalar baseline is `@workgroup(1,1,1)` (one invocation per query row, severely GPU-underutilized) while the coopmat kernel is `@workgroup(32,1,1)` over a 16-row query TILE, PLUS coopmat QKᵀ. So it is a tile-parallelism + coopmat-QKᵀ combined win over a deliberately-serial baseline (faster-than-OUR-scalar, an honest first attention-TFLOPS — NOT a tensor-core-only figure, NOT a llama A/B). The remaining Amdahl ceiling (un-accelerated scalar P·V over 1024 outputs, the 16-lane softmax, the S/P shared round-trips) is exactly what the DEFERRED coopmat-PV Option C targets — this milestone de-risks it by proving the QKᵀ + acc-in-shared mechanism CORRECT. New artifacts: `examples/flash_attention_coopmat.axc`; AT-2200/2201/2206 in `compile_shared_examples.rs`; AT-2202/2203/2205 in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs` (reusing `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference`); the AT-2204 A/B bench `resident_flash_attention.rs`.*

*(Status: IMPLEMENTED — Coder, off Architect r2 [after dual design review: optimistic APPROVE, pessimistic APPROVE]. PROPOSED design: Design spec → `.pipeline/runs/M3.2c-perf-architect.json` (verdict PROPOSED). r2 PINS, at the lane→cell + barrier-placement level the Coder needs to be race-free (this repo's FIRST per-lane divergent-work kernel): R1 both work-partitions (softmax lane=row L<16; P·V/zero-init/finalize cell c→lane c%32) DISJOINT+TOTAL; R2 the exact 4-barrier sequence at conditional_depth==0 (NONE inside the lane<16 branch) with barrier(2) ordering the warp-collective S-store→scalar-read and barrier(3) widened to cover the corr_sh cross-lane write/read; R3 s_acc declared-inside-the-loop, iteration-local, AT-2201 asserts 0 coopmat OpPhi. Shared budget reconciled to 13504 B incl corr_sh. The ≥3× perf gate is re-framed as a STRETCH — per Amdahl a ~1.3-2× honest-negative is the EXPECTED, documented, non-failure outcome; the PRIMARY deliverable is the correctness+mechanism proof. CRUX (acc-rescale) resolved = Option A (acc-in-shared, pure source); Option C (coopmat-acc diagonal-scale) DEFERRED as a bounded new feature. ZERO codegen change — coopmat QKᵀ via the existing shared-source coopmat load/store (`coopmat.rs:315/358`) + `exp()` (M3.2c-exp) + acc-in-shared (M3.2b). New artifacts: `examples/flash_attention_coopmat.axc` (pure source) + GPU dispatch/correctness tests in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs` (reusing `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference`) + a resident bench `resident_flash_attention.rs` (scalar-vs-coopmat speedup + per-phase breakdown) + a CI compile/streaming anchor in `compile_shared_examples.rs`. AT-2200..2207. FROZEN 1e-3 gate (never loosened); ≥3×-faster-than-scalar STRETCH perf gate with an honest-negative clause; NO competitive-with-llama claim.)*

## 3.1.31 M4.1 Phase 4 — FlashAttention as a `torch.library` custom-op through the zero-copy frontend [MERGED — completes the dual-kernel PyTorch adoption story]

M4.1 Phases 1–3 (§3.1.25–§3.1.27) built the CUDA↔Vulkan zero-copy PyTorch frontend and registered the **Q4_K_M matmul** as a first-class `torch.ops.axiom.q4km_matmul` op (`torch.library.custom_op` + `register_fake`, composing under `torch.compile`). **Phase 4 applies the SAME proven frontend pattern to a SECOND kernel** — the merged coopmat FlashAttention leader (`examples/flash_attention_coopmat.axc`, §3.1.30) — registering `torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention(Tensor q, Tensor k, Tensor v, int seq_len, int head_dim) -> Tensor`. Now BOTH a matmul AND an attention kernel are callable from PyTorch from a single annotated source → portable SPIR-V → a first-class op. **This is the SAME frontend pattern, not a new mechanism: it REUSES (does not reinvent) the R1/R2/R3 + PCR-1 machinery wholesale.** NO production codegen/runtime change.

**Op signature + `register_fake` (the q4km pattern, retargeted).** Schema `(Tensor q, Tensor k, Tensor v, int seq_len, int head_dim) -> Tensor`, `mutates_args=()`. `seq_len`/`head_dim` are caller-supplied ints (mirroring q4km's `M,N,K`, `ops.py:255`) so `register_fake` returns `q.new_empty((seq_len, head_dim), torch.float32)` WITHOUT touching the registry (FakeTensorMode has no device memory). The real impl R1-validates `q/k/v.shape == (seq_len, head_dim)`, `head_dim==64`, `seq_len%16==0`, CUDA + f32 + contiguous, BEFORE any dispatch, then clones the `[seq,d]` f32 O view — provably `(shape,dtype,device)`-identical to the fake meta (no `torch.compile` miscompile, the AT-2111c property reapplied as AT-2122). Explicit ints (not inferred) for the SAME reason as q4km (`ops.py:248-251`): `from __future__ import annotations` stringifies annotations that `infer_schema` rejects, and the ints trace as graph constants making the op a clean compiled LEAF.

**The 4-zero-copy-buffer SharedSession plan (SIMPLER than q4km — no upload-once).** UNLIKE q4km (q upload-once + x/C zero-copy = a split), FlashAttention has NO static weights — Q, K, V are ALL per-call data + O is the per-call output → **all 4 are per-call ZERO-COPY exportable shared buffers**: buffer 0 `Q[seq,d] f32`, buffer 1 `K[seq,d] f32`, buffer 2 `V[seq,d] f32`, buffer 3 `O[seq,d] f32` (all `4·seq·d` bytes; 50 GB cap sums all four, CLAUDE.md hard rule 1). The kernel binds EXACTLY 4 SSBOs `{Q,K,V,O}` (`flash_attention_coopmat.axc:66-70`; AT-2206 seq-invariant); `FlashAttention.__init__` asserts `buffer_count()==4` (the q4km `q4km.py:223` analogue). `allocate_shared(sizes=[qsz,ksz,vsz,osz])` (`__init__.py:229`) imports each fd generically (`__init__.py:268-272`) — q4km proved 3 buffers; 4 is the same code path + one entry (RISK R-A). Dispatch is staging-free (`dispatch_zero_copy`, `__init__.py:342`, 0 staging / 0 `vkCmdCopyBuffer`, the AT-2112 proof reused as AT-2125). **Layout match (load-bearing, cited):** the kernel reads Q/K/V/O as CONTIGUOUS row-major `[seq, head_dim]` f32 — `Q[g_row*head_dim+d]` (`:97`), `K[g_krow*head_dim+d_full]` (`:127`), `V[g_vrow*head_dim+vd]` (`:137`), `O[g_orow*head_dim+dd]` (`:242`) — so a torch contiguous `[seq,d]` f32 tensor IS the zero-copy layout (no transpose). **Dispatch geometry** `workgroups=(seq//16,1,1)` (`q_tile=gid(0)/32`, `:22-25`); push constants `{seq_len, head_dim, inv_sqrt_d}` by name (`assemble_push_constants`, `__init__.py:185`), with `inv_sqrt_d = 1/sqrt(head_dim)` computed in Python and packed as an IEEE-754 f32 via the **r2 f32-push-constant fix** (next paragraph) — the ONE new push-constant detail (q4km had only int scalars; RISK R-D, now CLOSED; the kernel applies the 1/sqrt(d) scale internally at `:180,:199`). The W1 contiguity/dtype/device guard is applied PER-INPUT to ALL THREE of q/k/v (a non-contiguous K/V with a matching `data_ptr` would be silent-wrong, the W1 class — guarding only one input leaves the others exposed).

**dtype = f32 (NOT f16, unlike q4km).** The coopmat kernel's Q/K/V params are `readonly_buffer[f32]` (`:64-66`) — it does the f32→f16 cast INTERNALLY (`f32_to_f16` when staging the coopmat A/B tiles, `:97,:127`; V stays f32 for the scalar P·V, `:137`). So the zero-copy buffers are f32 and R1 requires `q/k/v.dtype==torch.float32` (the q4km f16 guard, flipped to f32). O is f32.

**The f32 push-constant fix (r2 — the ONE blocker resolved, FRONTEND-only, NO codegen change).** The existing push-constant path is INTEGER-ONLY and cannot pack an f32: `assemble_push_constants` (`__init__.py:208`) does `val = int(values[name])` UNCONDITIONALLY → `int(0.125)==0` → four zero bytes → the kernel reads `inv_sqrt_d=0.0` → uniform softmax → WRONG attention; and `CompiledKernel.scalar_layout` (`crates/axc-py/src/lib.rs:85-92`) returns `(name, offset, size_bytes)` and DISCARDS `slot.ty`, so f32 and u32 both report size 4 and Python cannot even detect inv_sqrt_d is f32. The HIR slot DOES carry `ScalarPushConstantSlot.ty: ScalarTy` (`crates/axc-hir/src/param.rs:96`; F32≠U32), so the fix SURFACES a field that already exists: **(1)** PyO3 `scalar_layout()` emits the scalar type per slot — a 4-tuple `(name, offset, size_bytes, ty_str)` via the existing `ScalarTy::display_name()` (`ty.rs:71-87`, returns `'f32'`/`'u32'`/…) — reading the HIR's existing `ScalarTy`, NO codegen change; **(2)** Python `assemble_push_constants` BRANCHES on `ty_str` — `struct.pack('<f', float(value))` for an f32 slot, the existing `int(value).to_bytes(...)` for an int slot — so `inv_sqrt_d=0.125` packs to the correct IEEE-754 bytes `0x3E000000`, not 0; **(3)** a fail-fast CPU unit assertion (**AT-2129**) asserts the assembled blob's inv_sqrt_d bytes `== struct.pack('<f', 1/sqrt(head_dim))` BEFORE the GPU gate, so an f32-pack regression fails fast/locally, not silently-wrong on the GPU. This is a BOUNDED FRONTEND change (PyO3 surfaces ty + Python branches on f32 + the CPU assert), NOT a codegen / runtime-kernel change — if the Coder finds it needs codegen, the binding_plan construction, or runtime dispatch, STOP + escalate (it must not — the HIR already carries `slot.ty`).

**Which kernel — the coopmat LEADER (default), scalar fallback opt-in.** Wire `flash_attention_coopmat.axc` (the headline). Constraints (R1-validated, host-preflight via the `fa2_coopmat_preflight` analogue, `dispatch_shared_m32.rs:2545-2548`): `head_dim==64` EXACTLY (CORE pin), `seq_len%16==0`, `@workgroup(32,1,1)`. `examples/flash_attention_exp.axc` (scalar, `head_dim<=64`, `@workgroup(1,1,1)`, ~28× slower per §3.1.30) is an explicit `kernel='scalar'` fallback (no silent switch). A Lavapipe `CoopMatUnsupported` is a TYPED SKIP (`dispatch_shared_m32.rs:2579`) — CI Lavapipe skips the coopmat correctness test; GPU correctness runs on NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 (the M4.1 target, as §3.1.30); a scalar-kernel correctness leg CAN anchor on Lavapipe (RISK R-G).

**The R1/R2/R3 + PCR-1 reuse (verbatim, cited — REUSE, NOT REINVENT).** **R1** fail-closed validation = the structure of `ops.py:277-300`, retargeted to (seq,head_dim) + 3 input tensors. **R3** on-stream + cross-stream hand-back = `ops.py:306-337` VERBATIM (the handshake + any device copy + `cv.clone()` inside `with torch.cuda.stream(session.stream)`, `ev_in` caller→S, `ev_out` S→caller; a single `ev_in` covers all three q/k/v under the DOCUMENTED same-caller-stream precondition — q, k AND v must all be produced on the SAME caller stream before the op call, the existing G-5 Phase-1 assumption, stated in the op docstring + AT-2123; identical to q4km's single-x `ev_in` under its same-stream precondition). **R2 + PCR-1** = `session.wait_completion()` bounded owned-output barrier (`__init__.py:360`) + `_recover_faulted_session` (`ops.py:340-409`) VERBATIM (device_wait_idle drain FIRST, conditional `release_cuda_wait` only if payload<V2, then poison+evict+rebuild) — the both-sides wait-release (AT-2114) reapplied as AT-2124. The registry lock spans the WHOLE op body (`ops.py:274`) so a concurrent release cannot tear down mid-dispatch and no two same-key op calls are in-flight (the `mutates_args=()` soundness invariant). The session registry is keyed `(device_index, seq_len, head_dim, kernel)` — the FA analogue of q4km's `(weights_id, N)` registry — with NO upload-once step (q4km's `upload_weights` has no FA analogue).

**`torch.compile` composition (eager-zero-copy / compile-device-copy honesty, the q4km contract).** `register_fake` supplies the `(seq,d)` f32 meta; `mutates_args=()` declares the op pure (reads q/k/v, returns a fresh clone; the SharedSession state is hidden behind the registry key, not aliased); the op returns a CLONE of O (never the persistent shared view) so a later call cannot torn-read. EAGER fast path: when q/k/v ARE the `flash_attention_views(...)` shared views (contiguous `[seq,d]` f32), 0/0 staging zero-copy (AT-2125). Under fullgraph `torch.compile`, Inductor functionalization may realize/clone inputs → `data_ptr` identity is lost → the op CORRECTLY falls to the device_copy path (disclosed, no host round-trip, `last_attn_dispatch_path()=='device_copy'`). The 0/0 zero-copy claim is EAGER-scoped; under compile the op is CORRECT but may device_copy (AT-2121 asserts 0 graph breaks + compiled==eager, records whichever path ran — NOT zero_copy under compile). The W1 contiguity+shape guard (`q4km.py:368-383`) is reused — a non-contiguous/transposed same-storage view on the zero-copy path is REJECTED, never silently reinterpreted (RISK R-E).

**Correctness oracle — torch SDPA f32, FROZEN 1e-3.** PRIMARY = a **torch SDPA f32 oracle** (`torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention` on f32 inputs, real f32 exp — APPLES-TO-APPLES with the kernel's GLSL.std.450 `exp()`, M3.2c-exp; force `SDPBackend.MATH` so the oracle is provably exact f32, or a manual f32 stable-softmax). FROZEN tol = COMBINED abs/rel **1e-3** per O element (the SAME gate as §3.1.30 AT-2202/2203). JUSTIFICATION: attention outputs are CONVEX COMBINATIONS of V (softmax weights ∈[0,1] summing to 1) — NOT cancellation-prone (unlike q4km's near-zero matmul outputs that needed a condition-aware metric); the only new error is the f16-input coopmat QKᵀ rounding, which the smooth bounded softmax damps — the kernel MEASURED 8.5e-5 (seq=512) / 5.1e-5 (seq=2048) vs the Rust true-exp oracle (§3.1.30), an order of magnitude under 1e-3. The gate is FROZEN — a miss is REPORTED as the f16-input QKᵀ limitation (a distinct honest-negative; fallback = the scalar kernel), NEVER loosened. SECONDARY cross-check (recommended, optional): promote `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference` (`dispatch_shared_m32.rs:1450`) to `pub axc_driver::fa2_oracle` (the `q4km_oracle` promotion pattern) + an optional PyO3 bridge, and assert op-vs-Rust-oracle ALSO ≤1e-3 — guards against a torch-SDPA-internal-math surprise (RISK R-C).

**Acceptance (HONEST, measured NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000; Lavapipe coopmat-typed-skip).** **AT-2120** correct within FROZEN 1e-3 vs torch SDPA (force `SDPBackend.MATH`) at seq=512 AND 2048, head_dim=64 (expected ~5-9e-5) — the Coder RE-MEASURES + REPORTS the op-vs-SDPA gap on THIS test's actual fixture (a sharper Gaussian softmax may widen the f16-QKᵀ gap vs the near-uniform 8.5e-5 fixture; if it nears 1e-3, REPORT it as the f16-QKᵀ limitation, NEVER loosen 1e-3 — margin >10×). **AT-2121** `torch.compile(fullgraph=True)` 0 graph breaks, compiled==eager. **AT-2122** R1 fail-closed (shape/head_dim/seq/dtype/device/contiguity, ALL THREE inputs). **AT-2123** R3 cross-stream correct (same-caller-stream precondition). **AT-2124** R2/PCR-1 both-sides wait-release + poison/rebuild (mirrors AT-2114). **AT-2125** eager zero-copy staging-free 0/0 for ALL FOUR buffers (q AND k AND v all written into their views, not just q). **AT-2126** opcheck + grad-tracking-raises. **AT-2127** the ≤10-line demo. **AT-2128** NO regression to the q4km op + saxpy + Phase 1-3. **AT-2129** CPU-only fail-fast f32-push-constant pack assert (inv_sqrt_d bytes == `struct.pack('<f', 1/sqrt(head_dim))`, NOT zero — guards the r2 §f32-fix before the GPU). **HONEST: NO beat-cuBLAS / beat-SDPA claim** — the attention kernel is NOT competitive (coopmat-PV DEFERRED, §3.1.30); the value is REAL torch integration of a SECOND kernel (the dual-kernel adoption story). FROZEN 1e-3 NEVER loosens.

**Top risks.** (R-A) the 4-buffer SharedSession reuse (q4km proved 3; the 4th-buffer fd-import/binding is unproven — AT-2125 confirms 4 fds + 0/0 staging; an integration finding, not a codegen change). (R-B) the rigid head_dim==64/seq%16==0/wg(32,1,1) constraints through a generic-looking op (R1 raises on every violation — no silent clamp). (R-C) torch SDPA backend non-determinism (force `SDPBackend.MATH` + cross-check the Rust f32 oracle). (R-D — CLOSED by the r2 f32-push-constant fix, was the pessimistic BLOCKER) the f32 `inv_sqrt_d` push constant: the existing path was integer-only (`int(0.125)==0`) and `scalar_layout()` dropped `slot.ty` so Python couldn't detect f32; FIXED FRONTEND-only (PyO3 surfaces ty + Python `struct.pack('<f',·)` branch + AT-2129 CPU fail-fast assert + AT-2120 end-to-end). (R-E) the `[seq,d]` layout match (the W1 contiguity guard reused). (R-F) which kernel (coopmat leader default + scalar opt-in). (R-G) Lavapipe has no coopmat (NVIDIA-gated correctness + a scalar-kernel Lavapipe anchor recommended).

*(Status: PROPOSED r2 — Architect, after dual design review [optimistic APPROVE; pessimistic NEEDS_REVISION → resolved]. The r2 fix addresses the ONE pessimistic blocker — f32 push-constant support for `inv_sqrt_d` — as a BOUNDED FRONTEND change (PyO3 `scalar_layout()` surfaces the slot `ScalarTy` + Python `assemble_push_constants` branches `struct.pack('<f',·)` on f32 + the AT-2129 CPU fail-fast assert), NOT a codegen/runtime change (the HIR already carries `slot.ty`). The five non-blocking notes are folded (AT-2125 all-4-buffers written, AT-2120 measured-gap re-confirmed/reported, the W1 contiguity guard applied to all three q/k/v, R3's same-caller-stream precondition documented, the optional `fa2_oracle` pub-mod cross-check). Everything else from r1 is unchanged. Design spec → `.pipeline/runs/M41p4-architect.json` (verdict PROPOSED, revision r2). The SECOND application of the M4.1 Phase-3 frontend pattern to a kernel (Q4_K_M matmul + FlashAttention both as `torch.ops.axiom.*` ops). Op `axiom::flash_attention(Tensor q, Tensor k, Tensor v, int seq_len, int head_dim) -> Tensor` via `torch.library.custom_op` + `register_fake` (`q.new_empty((seq,d),f32)` meta) + a CUDA impl driving a `(device,seq,head_dim,kernel)`-keyed cached 4-buffer `SharedSession`. 4 per-call ZERO-COPY buffers (q,k,v,O — NO upload-once, simpler than q4km); coopmat LEADER kernel wired (head_dim==64, seq%16==0, wg(32,1,1)), scalar fallback opt-in; f32 q/k/v in (kernel casts to f16 internally); torch SDPA f32 oracle (force `SDPBackend.MATH`) within FROZEN 1e-3 (convex-combination justified; optional Rust `fa2_oracle` cross-check); R1/R2/R3 + PCR-1 REUSED VERBATIM (`ops.py:277-409`); `torch.compile(fullgraph=True)` composition with the EAGER-zero-copy / compile-device-copy honesty. New artifacts: `py/axiom_compute/flash_attention.py` (the `FlashAttention` wrapper, the `Q4KMMatmul` twin), `py/axiom_compute/attention_ops.py` (the op surface, the `ops.py` twin), `py/examples/flash_attention_torch_compile_demo.py`, `py/tests/test_torch_library_flash_attention.py` (AT-2120..2129); the REQUIRED r2 frontend f32-push-constant fix in `crates/axc-py/src/lib.rs` (`scalar_layout()` 4-tuple) + `py/axiom_compute/__init__.py` (`assemble_push_constants` f32 branch); optional `crates/axc-driver/src/fa2_oracle.rs` (the promoted true-exp reference) + a PyO3 bridge. NO production codegen/runtime change (the r2 fix is FRONTEND-only — a binding accessor + a Python packer branch); FROZEN 1e-3 untouched; NO beat-cuBLAS/SDPA claim. ## 3.1.32 M3.2c-PV — coopmat-accelerated P·V for FlashAttention [MERGED]

M3.2c-perf (§3.1.30, MERGED — `examples/flash_attention_coopmat.axc`) accelerated `S = Q·Kᵀ` on tensor cores (coopmat → shared S) but left the online-softmax + **P·V + acc-rescale SCALAR** over the shared tile. The honest Amdahl ceiling = the scalar P·V (16 MACs × 1024 outputs, 32 lanes) + the 16-lane softmax + the S/P shared round-trips; `acc` is held in `shared[f32,16*64]` (NOT a coopmat — the crux: `coopmat_mul_add` is A·B+C only, no per-row diagonal rescale). **M3.2c-PV accelerates the SECOND matmul `P·V` on tensor cores the SAME way QKᵀ was — coopmat-into-shared + scalar-combine — WITHOUT making `acc` a coopmat (which would re-introduce the crux that DEFERRED Option C).** New file `examples/flash_attention_coopmat_pv.axc` (the M3.2c-perf kernel STAYS as the no-regression baseline + the A/B opponent).

**PURE SOURCE — the load-bearing decision (cited, CONFIRMED by reading the code).** P·V with BOTH operands loaded from SHARED is the IDENTICAL mechanism to QKᵀ: `emit_coopmat_load_shared_inline` (`coopmat.rs:315`, the SINGLE-index Workgroup-class access chain, AT-1614) loads BOTH P (f16 A-operand from `p_sh`) and V (f16 B-operand from `v_tile_sh`), exactly as QKᵀ loads Q (A) and Kᵀ (B) from shared today (`flash_attention_coopmat.axc:149-157`); `emit_coopmat_mul_add` (`coopmat.rs:173`, `OpCooperativeMatrixMulAddKHR` = A·B+C) accumulates into a FRESH iteration-local `pv_acc = coopmat_zero()` (`coopmat.rs:151`, `OpConstantNull`); `emit_coopmat_store_shared_inline` (`coopmat.rs:358`) stores PV→`pv_sh`, IDENTICAL to the S-store (`flash_attention_coopmat.axc:165`). `body.rs` already wires BOTH the shared coopmat load (`body.rs:1474-1500`, `CoopMatLoadSource::Shared`) and store (`body.rs:667-686`). `derive_coopmat_shape` (`lower.rs:447`) derives the kernel's SINGLE coopmat shape from the FIRST `coopmat_mul_add`; since BOTH QKᵀ and PV use the identical **16×16×16 f16/f16/f32/f32** shape (`matrix[f16,16,16,a]` · `matrix[f16,16,16,b]` → `matrix[f32,16,16,accumulator]`, the M3.5b mixed shape the kernel already declares), the runtime records EXACTLY ONE shape that satisfies BOTH matmuls (`coopmat_required_shape_from_meta`, runtime `coopmat.rs:393`) — NO new metadata, NO second shape. **CONCLUSION: ZERO new builtin / capability / typecheck / emit path.** The ONLY genuine constraint is a SOURCE constraint (not a codegen gap): P must be materialized as a SEPARATE f16 shared tile `p_sh` (the coopmat A-operand is f16, like Q/K) — M3.2c-perf overwrote S-as-P IN PLACE in the f32 `s_tile_sh`, which CANNOT be a coopmat A-operand. So the softmax writes `p_sh[r*16+t] = f32_to_f16(p)` instead of back into `s_tile_sh`; and V narrows to f16 (a coopmat B-operand). Both are SOURCE changes, NOT codegen features.

**The mechanism (the QKᵀ pattern, applied to P·V).** (1) S=Q·Kᵀ coopmat → `s_tile_sh` (UNCHANGED). (2) Scalar online-softmax (CHANGED: P written as **f16 into `p_sh`**, NOT in-place into the f32 S). (3) **NEW: PV = P·V coopmat.** P is [16,16], V is [16,64] → PV is [16,64] = **4 output sub-blocks of 16×16**; the contraction is over TILE_K=16 = exactly 1 coopmat K-block per sub-block. Load P once (`coopmat_load(p_sh,0,16)`), and for each of the 4 head-dim sub-blocks `ds`: a fresh `pv_acc_ds = coopmat_zero()`, `vb_ds = coopmat_load(v_tile_sh, ds*16, 64)` (V[16][64] row-major, the ds-th 16-wide column slice), `pv_acc_ds = coopmat_mul_add(pa, vb_ds, pv_acc_ds)`, `coopmat_store(pv_acc_ds → pv_sh, ds*16, 64)`. (4) **Scalar rescale-add into acc-in-shared (UNCHANGED mechanism, reads `pv_sh` instead of computing the dot):** `acc_sh[c] = acc_sh[c]*corr_sh[r] + pv_sh[c]`, lane L owns cells {c=L+32·i}. The per-row diagonal rescale stays SCALAR (acc stays in shared) — **the crux is NOT re-introduced. SAME 'coopmat-into-shared + scalar-combine' as QKᵀ.**

**Shared layout — 16064 B < 16384 portable floor (320 B headroom).** q f16 2048 + k f16 2048 + **v f16 2048** (narrowed from f32 4096 — V is now a coopmat B-operand, NOT a scalar-dot source) + s f32 1024 + **p_sh f16 512** (NEW) + **pv_sh f32 4096** (NEW) + acc f32 4096 + m/l/corr 192 = **16064 B** (Δ +2560 vs 13504; the V f16-narrowing funds p_sh + pv_sh back under the floor). seq-invariant (streaming preserved); exactly {Q,K,V,O} bound (AT-2216). No double-buffer/bigger tiles (DEFERRED).

**Lane partitions (R1, carried) + the 5 barriers (R2, ALL at conditional_depth==0).** R1: softmax lane L owns row L (L<16, NOW writes `p_sh` f16 instead of overwriting S — the only R1 change); rescale/zero/finalize lane L owns cells {c=L+32·i}; the coopmat P·V is warp-collective (the runtime owns the lane→element map). **Barriers** (one MORE than M3.2c-perf's 4 — the PV→shared round-trip adds one; the BarrierInDivergentContext guard forbids depth>0): (1) staging → coopmat loads; (2) S-store → scalar S-read; **(P)** after BOTH `p_sh` AND `corr_sh` written → before the coopmat P-load (reads p_sh) AND the rescale's corr_sh read — **WIDENED to cover p_sh** (warp-collective coopmat-A load, written by lane r<16) AND the corr_sh cross-lane (written by lane r, read by cell-owner lane c%32); **(PV) NEW** — after the warp-collective PV→shared store → before the scalar rescale reads `pv_sh` (a miss = partially-stored PV = silent wrong, the M3.7 class); (4) WAR end-of-body (widened to cover p_sh/pv_sh). ALL 5 at conditional_depth==0, NONE inside the lane<16 branch.

**R3 — s_acc AND pv_acc BOTH iteration-local (NO phi).** BOTH `s_acc` (4 QKᵀ mul_adds) AND every `pv_acc_ds` (4 PV sub-blocks) are declared INSIDE the K/V loop body (fresh `coopmat_zero()` each, mul_adds UNROLLED at top level, NOT nested) → NO pre-loop SSA id → `detect_loop_carried_coopmat` (`body.rs:969`) makes NO phi → genuinely iteration-local. **CONFIRMED both work:** each is a distinct fresh `OpConstantNull` per iteration; they share the cached coopmat-accumulator TYPE id (one 16×16 f32 type) but each let-binding gets its own `constant_null` id (`coopmat.rs:161`) — no aliasing, no carry. AT-2211 asserts the K/V loop header has EXACTLY 0 coopmat OpPhi (the falsifier for an accidental carry of EITHER) + the op-shape (3 coopmat types [shape REUSED, not doubled], 13 loads = 4 Q + 4 K + 1 P + 4 V, 5 stores = 1 S + 4 PV, 8 mul_adds = 4 QKᵀ + 4 PV, 5 OpConstantNull). The M3.6 nested-coopmat-carry + AT-1708 conditional-reassign traps are AVOIDED BY CONSTRUCTION.

**Numerics — FROZEN 1e-3, a SECOND new error source.** Same FA2 online softmax + same real `exp()`. The NEW error sources: f16(P) AND f16(V) in the coopmat P·V (beyond the QKᵀ f16). Attention outputs are CONVEX COMBINATIONS of V (softmax weights ∈[0,1] sum 1, NOT cancellation-prone); P ∈ [0,1] (post-exp), V ~O(1) → the smooth bounded softmax damps the rounding — expected ~5–9e-5 like the QKᵀ f16 (which measured 8.5e-5/5.1e-5, §3.1.30). FROZEN combined abs/rel **1e-3** per O element vs `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference` (`dispatch_shared_m32.rs:1450`) at seq=512 (AT-2212) AND 2048 (AT-2213). NEVER loosened — a miss is REPORTED as the f16-PV limitation (a distinct honest-negative; fallback = the M3.2c-perf scalar-PV kernel).

**Acceptance (HONEST, NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000; Lavapipe coopmat-typed-skip).** PRIMARY (gating) = CORRECTNESS + MECHANISM: AT-2210 (spirv-val clean, caps == the M3.2c-perf set, GLSL.std.450 once), AT-2211 (BOTH s_acc + pv_acc iteration-local → 0 coopmat OpPhi + the op-shape, n_types==3 proving the shape is reused), AT-2216 (shared==16064 B < 16384; seq-invariant; {Q,K,V,O}), AT-2212/2213 (FROZEN 1e-3 vs the true-exp oracle at seq=512/2048), AT-2215 (cross-kernel agreement vs the scalar-PV kernel within 1e-3 — two coopmat-QKᵀ impls differing ONLY in the P·V path, the structural-race/wrong-stride/f16-PV detector). STRETCH (falsifiable, NOT primary) = AT-2214 (FASTER than the scalar-PV kernel at seq=2048 — report the ratio + a per-phase breakdown + an honest attention-TFLOPS). NO-REGRESSION: the M3.2c-perf AT-2200..2206 ALL still pass (the scalar-PV leader is UNTOUCHED — this milestone ADDS a file).

**HONEST-NEGATIVE clause (M3.7/M3.8 discipline — a REAL possibility, faster-than-scalar-PV is the LIKELY honest-negative).** The scalar P·V in M3.2c-perf was a SMALL inner product (16 MACs/output) — maybe ALREADY cheap relative to the softmax + the shared round-trips. The coopmat P·V ADDS a P→shared(f16) write + a coopmat P-load + 4 V-loads + 4 mul_adds + a PV→shared store + a pv_sh read + 2 extra barriers + the f16 narrowing. **If the coopmat P·V does NOT beat the scalar P·V, REPORT IT HONESTLY** (both wall-times, the ratio, the QKᵀ/softmax/PV/rescale phase breakdown, a diagnosis) — a DOCUMENTED FINDING, NOT a milestone failure: the PRIMARY deliverable (correctness + the coopmat-PV-from-shared MECHANISM) stands, and the M3.2c-perf scalar-PV kernel REMAINS leader. NO competitive-with-llama claim. FROZEN 1e-3 NEVER loosens; the faster-than-scalar-PV bar is NOT loosened to manufacture a win.

**Phasing.** **CORE (this milestone):** coopmat PV=P·V→shared (P f16 from p_sh, V f16 from v_tile_sh, 4 sub-blocks → pv_sh) + scalar rescale-add (acc-in-shared, UNCHANGED). **DEFERRED:** Option C (coopmat acc + per-row diagonal-scale — removes the LAST scalar pass, the rescale; still needs the diagonal-scale NEW codegen feature); softmax on tensor cores; TILE_Q>16 / multi-subgroup; head_dim>64; double-buffered staging; the llama.cpp flash-attention A/B.

**Top risks.** (1) PERF HONEST-NEGATIVE (the LIKELY outcome) — the extra round-trip + f16 narrowing may ERASE the small scalar-PV win (mitigated by AT-2214's breakdown; the scalar-PV kernel stays leader). (2) f16-PV numerics — a SECOND new error source (could exceed 1e-3; damped by convex-combination; fallback scalar-PV). (3) the P-from-shared coopmat-A LAYOUT (p_sh f16 RowMajor stride 16; a wrong stride/transpose silently corrupts PV — AT-2215 detects). (4) the V coopmat-B sub-block strides (offset ds*16, stride 64; AT-2215 detects). (5) BARRIER(P)/BARRIER(PV) placement (both NEW/widened; a miss = partially-written p_sh/pv_sh = silent wrong; AT-2215 + the differing-rowmax fixture detect). (6) M3.6/AT-1708 coopmat-loop traps — AVOIDED by construction (both accumulators iteration-local; AT-2211 pins 0 coopmat OpPhi). (7) pv_acc + s_acc both iteration-local — CONFIRMED works (distinct constant_null ids, shared type id). (8) 16064 B is TIGHTER than M3.2c-perf (320 B headroom; achieved by narrowing v_tile_sh to f16; no double-buffer).

**Implemented (M3.2c-PV — measured on NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell).** `examples/flash_attention_coopmat_pv.axc` compiles + **spirv-val clean**; caps byte-EQUAL to the M3.2c-perf scalar-PV kernel (NO new capability), GLSL.std.450 once (AT-2210). **EXACTLY 0 coopmat OpPhi** (both `s_acc` + the 4 `pv_acc` iteration-local) + the exact op-shape: 3 coopmat types (shape REUSED), 13 loads (4 Q + 4 K + 1 P + 4 V), 5 stores (1 S + 4 PV), 8 mul_adds (4 QKᵀ + 4 PV), 5 OpConstantNull (AT-2211). Shared == **16064 B** < 16384 (AT-2216). CORRECTNESS vs the true-exp oracle (differing-rowmax fixture, post-max arg ~15) within FROZEN 1e-3 — the **MEASURED f16-PV max_diff = 8.18e-5 (seq=512) / 5.27e-5 (seq=2048)**, essentially the SAME as the QKᵀ-only f16 (8.5e-5/5.1e-5) → the f16(P)·f16(V) SECOND error source is absorbed by the convex-combination softmax, an order under 1e-3 (AT-2212/2213). Cross-kernel vs the scalar-PV kernel: 1.25e-5 (seq=512) / 5.4e-6 (seq=2048) (AT-2215). **PERF (AT-2214, NOT the expected honest-negative — a MEASURED WIN): coopmat-PV = 0.250 ms vs the M3.2c-perf scalar-PV 0.346 ms = 1.385× faster** (attention-TFLOPS 3.10 → 4.29), reproduced across runs — the coopmat P·V removed enough scalar ALU (the 16-MAC dot over 1024 outputs) to net-win despite the p_sh-f16 write + coopmat P/V loads + PV→shared store + 2 extra barriers. The M3.2c-perf AT-2200..2206 ALL still pass (no-regression; the scalar-PV kernel UNTOUCHED). FROZEN 1e-3 never loosened; honest report. clippy clean; full `cargo test --workspace` green.

*(Status: IMPLEMENTED — Coder, off Architect [dual design review: optimistic APPROVE, pessimistic APPROVE]. Design spec → `.pipeline/runs/M32cpv-architect.json`. PURE SOURCE — ZERO codegen change: the QKᵀ pattern (`coopmat_load`-shared `coopmat.rs:315`, `coopmat_mul_add` `:173`, `coopmat_store`-shared `:358`, `coopmat_zero` `:151`) applied to P·V; the single derived 16×16×16 f16/f16/f32/f32 shape (`lower.rs:447`) serves BOTH matmuls (no new metadata). New SOURCE constructs only: a `p_sh` f16 tile (the softmax writes P f16 instead of overwriting the f32 S — the coopmat A-operand constraint), an f16 V staging (coopmat B-operand), the coopmat PV block (4 sub-blocks), and BARRIER(PV). Both s_acc + pv_acc iteration-local → 0 coopmat OpPhi (AT-2211). Shared 16064 B < 16384 floor (V-f16-narrowing funds p_sh+pv_sh). FROZEN 1e-3 (never loosened); faster-than-scalar-PV is a STRETCH gate with an explicit HONEST-NEGATIVE (the LIKELY outcome — the scalar P·V may already be cheap; report the ratio + per-phase breakdown either way); the M3.2c-perf scalar-PV kernel STAYS leader; NO competitive-with-llama claim. New artifacts: `examples/flash_attention_coopmat_pv.axc` (pure source) + GPU correctness/cross-kernel tests in `dispatch_shared_m32.rs` (reusing `fa2_true_exp_softmax_reference` + the differing-rowmax fixture) + an A/B bench leg in `resident_flash_attention.rs` + compile/structural anchors in `compile_shared_examples.rs`. AT-2210..2216.)*

## 3.1.33 M4.3 — a `torch.compile` custom backend that auto-lowers matching SDPA to the AXIOM kernel [the M4 ADOPTION CAPSTONE — auto-routing, not explicit op calls]

M4.1 (§3.1.25–§3.1.27, §3.1.31) made the AXIOM Q4_K_M matmul AND FlashAttention callable from PyTorch as **explicit** `torch.ops.axiom.*` ops that compose under `torch.compile`. **M4.3 is the adoption capstone: AUTO-routing.** A user writes a normal PyTorch model with `scaled_dot_product_attention` and opts in via `torch.compile(model, backend="axiom")`; the backend walks the Dynamo FX graph, pattern-matches the SDPA nodes it can lower, rewrites ONLY those to `torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention`, and **delegates everything else (and every non-matching SDPA) to the default Inductor backend** — so the user's model auto-uses AXIOM where applicable, with NO behavior change and NO graph break elsewhere. **PYTHON-ONLY: NO Rust/`.axc`/SPIR-V/codegen/runtime change** — a `torch._dynamo.register_backend` fn + an FX graph pass that REUSES the already-merged `torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention` op (`py/axiom_compute/attention_ops.py:207-300`).

**HONEST framing (load-bearing, repeated in the demo + every docstring).** This is a **CAPABILITY demonstration, NOT a perf win.** torch's native SDPA dispatches to FlashAttention / memory-efficient / a fused MATH path — all FASTER than AXIOM's coopmat-QKᵀ + scalar-softmax + coopmat-PV kernel (which is itself NOT a perf leader, §3.1.32). **Auto-routing SDPA→AXIOM makes the model SLOWER than native.** The value is that AXIOM CAN register as a `torch.compile` backend, auto-lower a matching op, and stay CORRECT in a real Dynamo/Inductor flow. **OPT-IN ONLY** (`backend="axiom"` is never a default); **NO beat claim**; no latency gate (latency reported as context only, clearly labeled slower).

**q4km is OUT of auto-lowering scope (stated honestly).** `torch.ops.axiom.q4km_matmul(x, weights_id, M, N, K)` needs a pre-packed Q4_K_M **int `weights_id`** from a prior `q4km_register_weights` (`ops.py`) — an int handle into a process registry, NOT a natural graph value. A torch `nn.Linear` carries f32/f16 weights, not Q4_K_M 144-byte superblocks; there is NO FX pattern that produces Q4_K_M-packed weights. So q4km STAYS an explicit-op-call kernel; M4.3 does not auto-lower it. (A future helper that rewrites a user-MARKED quantized linear would NOT be graph-pattern auto-lowering and is out of scope here.)

**The backend mechanism (torch 2.12).** `torch._dynamo.register_backend(name="axiom", compiler_fn=axiom_backend)` at import (idempotent, guarded for the no-torch surface — the SAME idiom as `register_flash_attention_op`, `attention_ops.py:186-221`). Dynamo calls `axiom_backend(gm: torch.fx.GraphModule, example_inputs) -> Callable`. The backend: (1) `_rewrite_sdpa_to_axiom(gm, example_inputs)` IN-PLACE rewrites matching SDPA nodes → per-head `torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention` calls, then `gm.graph.lint()` + `gm.recompile()`; (2) **DELEGATES the rewritten graph to the DEFAULT backend** — `return torch._inductor.compile(gm, example_inputs)`. The AXIOM op nodes are opaque registered-custom-op calls (with a `register_fake` meta, `attention_ops.py:215-219`, so Inductor traces shapes + emits an extern/fallback call); everything NOT rewritten is compiled by Inductor exactly as the default backend would (IDENTICAL behavior). A backend (not a pre-dispatch pass / decomposed-pattern `subgraph_rewriter`) is chosen because at the Dynamo backend boundary SDPA is a SINGLE `call_function` node (`F.scaled_dot_product_attention` / `aten.scaled_dot_product_attention.default`) — NOT yet decomposed into `softmax(QKᵀ/√d)·V` (that happens later, inside Inductor) — so matching one node is far more robust than fuzzy-matching a softmax-matmul chain. Hard fallback to `gm.forward` (eager of the rewritten graph) if `torch._inductor.compile` is unavailable/raises (disclosed, still correct).

**The SDPA match predicate (fire ONLY when provably correct; ANY failure → leave the node untouched → Inductor handles it, no break, no error). r2: the flag extraction is PROVABLY FAIL-CLOSED against the POSITIONAL form.** A node matches iff ALL hold. **(P0) UNKNOWN-FORM FALLBACK (checked FIRST).** The schema is `scaled_dot_product_attention(query, key, value, attn_mask=None, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False, *, scale=None, enable_gqa=False)` — 6 positional-or-keyword slots, then keyword-ONLY scale/enable_gqa. Fall back (no match) if `node.op != "call_function"`, OR the target is not exactly in the known SDPA set (P1), OR `len(node.args) > 6` (an unexpected/future form), OR any kwarg key is outside `{attn_mask, dropout_p, is_causal, scale, enable_gqa}`. **(P1)** target ∈ {`F.scaled_dot_product_attention` (== `torch._C._nn.scaled_dot_product_attention`, same object), `aten.scaled_dot_product_attention.default`}; the Coder's first spike re-confirms it on the in-env torch. **(P2) FLAG EXTRACTION via the single fail-closed helper `_get_sdpa_arg` (THE r2 blocker fix).** Each POSITIONAL-OR-KEYWORD flag is read `args[idx] if len(args)>idx else kwargs.get(name, default)`: `attn_mask`=idx 3, `dropout_p`=idx 4, `is_causal`=idx 5. Each KEYWORD-ONLY flag (`scale`, `enable_gqa`) is read from kwargs ONLY (never positional). This DEFEATS the silent-wrong-math hole: `F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q,k,v,None,0.0,True)` ⇒ `args=(q,k,v,None,0.0,True)`, `kwargs={}`, and `is_causal` is READ from `args[5]==True` ⇒ NO MATCH (a kwargs-only reader would read the `False` default and silently lower a CAUSAL SDPA to FULL non-causal attention = WRONG); likewise a POSITIONAL mask `F.sdpa(q,k,v,mask)` ⇒ `attn_mask` read from `args[3]` ⇒ NO MATCH. MATCH requires (READ, not by omission): `attn_mask is None` AND `dropout_p==0.0` AND `is_causal is False` AND `enable_gqa is False` (secondary — see the PRIMARY GQA guard in P4). **(P2b)** `scale` is None OR `==1/sqrt(D)` — the kernel applies `inv_sqrt_d=1/sqrt(head_dim)` INTERNALLY (`flash_attention.py:301`), so a differing custom scale changes the math ⇒ no match. **(P3)** f32 q/k/v (the op requires f32, `attention_ops.py:243-246`). **(P4) SHAPE + the FOREGROUNDED GQA GUARD.** meta shape `[B,H,S,D]` with `D==64` EXACTLY (the coopmat CORE pin, `flash_attention.py:56,153-158`), `S%16==0` (`flash_attention.py:57,165-169`), and — **the PRIMARY GQA/MQA defense** — `tuple(q.shape)==tuple(k.shape)==tuple(v.shape)` as FULL 4-tuples (B,H,S,D all equal). GQA/MQA (K/V with FEWER heads than Q, `[B,Hkv,S,D]` with `Hkv<Hq`) FAILS this full-4-tuple equality on H ⇒ NO MATCH (this shapes-agree check is the real GQA guard — the `enable_gqa` flag is only defense-in-depth; the per-head loop assumes `Hq==Hk==Hv==H` and the equal-shapes requirement enforces exactly that). Shapes must be STATIC ints (a symbolic/dynamic S or D ⇒ no match). **(P5)** `not requires_grad` (the op is inference-only — `register_fake` has no backward); a non-Tensor/None meta ⇒ no match. **Meta read pinned to `example_value` FIRST (r2):** shape/dtype/requires_grad are read via the key chain `['example_value','val','tensor_meta']` — at the Dynamo BACKEND boundary the FakeTensor lives in `arg.meta['example_value']` (`val`/`tensor_meta` are ABSENT there, verified live), so `example_value` MUST be tried first or the predicate reads None for every shape and NEVER matches (the op would never fire). Missing meta on all keys ⇒ no match. The predicate is CONSERVATIVE — any uncertainty falls back. A false-positive lowering a causal/masked/differently-scaled SDPA would be SILENT WRONG MATH (RISK R-D), so every constraint must provably hold. **The extractor + predicate are PURE PYTHON, CPU-TESTABLE (AT-2149) — the positional-form hole is caught without a GPU.**

**The [B,H,S,D] → single-head [S,D] handling (the load-bearing rewrite).** torch SDPA is `[B,H,S,D]`; the AXIOM op is SINGLE-HEAD `[S,D]` (`flash_attention.py:268`, `attention_ops.py:247`). The rewrite LOOPS over `(b,h)`: `reshape` q/k/v to `[B*H,S,D]` once (no-op on contiguous), `select(0, b*H+h)` → a `[S,D]` view, `.contiguous()` (the op requires contiguous `[S,D]`, `flash_attention.py:236-240,254`), `o_head = torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention(qhead, khead, vhead, S, D)`, then `torch.stack` the `B*H` outputs → `[B*H,S,D]` → `reshape` → `[B,H,S,D]`. This is expressible for ANY static `B,H` (the loop unrolls into `B*H` op nodes); a **bound `AXIOM_SDPA_MAX_HEADS` (default 64)** caps the unroll (`B*H` over the bound ⇒ no match, fall back); symbolic B or H ⇒ no match. The per-head math is EXACTLY non-causal SDPA with the default scale; stacking reproduces SDPA's layout — a wrong index/stack would permute heads (RISK R-B, caught by the `[2,4]` 1e-3 test, AT-2146). The ONLY numerical difference is the kernel's internal f16 coopmat QKᵀ/PV rounding.

**Correctness anchor — FROZEN 1e-3 vs eager AND vs the default Inductor backend.** The lowered model output must match the ORIGINAL within combined abs/rel **1e-3** per element: PRIMARY vs `model(x)` EAGER (torch's native SDPA), CROSS-CHECK vs `torch.compile(model, backend="inductor")(x)` (the swap changed nothing beyond the op's numerics). This is the SAME 1e-3 gate as §3.1.31/§3.1.32 — the milestone REUSES `torch.ops.axiom.flash_attention`, inheriting the op's PROVEN numerics (measured ~5–9e-5). **NEVER loosened** — a miss is REPORTED as the f16-coopmat QKᵀ/PV limitation, and the fix is to NOT match (Inductor handles it), never to relax the gate.
